## Bootstrap preparation 1 — not launched

- Actual game: `1.6.4871 rev590`; Assembly-CSharp SHA256 `5CF1B5BE399D5B1C9C56CA72C9D35B4ECF307FEACF5859D04AC5A1AA5926356A`.

- **Pre-launch review failed:** protected Config/Saves paths were inferred through `Environment.GetFolderPath(UserProfile)`, which under the tool sandbox returns `C:\Users\CodexSandboxOffline`, not the player's profile. - No gameplay result, feedback resolution or BG acceptance evidence is claimed.

## Bootstrap preparation 2 — not launched

- Run ID: `4e2a9cdfb6ee42e18dc26ee66a9af29b`; same Workshop/game/initial harness identities as preparation 1.

- Source review approves the actual game's command-line syntax and direct `Application.Quit` approach. It is not gameplay approval or proof that any feedback issue is fixed.

Its only review candidates are two early native Mono fallback-load messages at Player.log lines 106–107. No other exception, patch or XML error was found.

- Version provenance correction: `Version.txt` says **1.6.4871 rev590**, but the executing game's startup line says **1.6.4871 rev591**. Both are recorded; the eight expected assembly hashes match. The file string must not be represented as the executing game's self-reported revision.

## Storage ownership baseline 1 — incomplete controls

- Run ID `84cab348bf0241308269ef7222e9212c`; Workshop HD v1.24.0, Harmony/Core/HD/harness only. Harness SHA256 `50E81FB8F0263A7EA7EF0FD02BDC4BA4649D669C0C503F67F219F8DF03E3FE0E`. Prepared 1,451 copied files with 63 protected player/installed-mod records; root reviewed all manifest roots and expected assembly identities.
- PID `33056`, start `2026-09-07T00:20:45.1214801Z`, subsequently observed exited. Actual map and expected assemblies initialized; adapter controls ran synchronously at tick 7.
- Own inventory control established actual `ThingOwner` custody, live production claim/evidence of 10, and unclaimed capacity 10. After that claim, published `IsDelivering=false`, `FreeUnitsFor=0`, patched `IsGoodStoreCell=false`: this is direct runtime reproduction of the own-inventory rejection. Own hands correctly returned true/10/allowed; foreign-owner inventory correctly returned false/0/blocked.
- The fourth control exposed a **fixture defect**: it assumed a spawned floor item's `holdingOwner` is null. The installed game registers spawned items in a map-owned `ThingOwner`; the actual observed `ParentHolder` was `Verse.Map`. The fixture stopped with a failed precondition and `inconclusive`, so the complete ten-control expectation was not reached.
- Root corrected that precondition to require actual `Spawned`, matching `Map`, and `ParentHolder == map`. No custody is fabricated by the correction. The complete fixture must be rerun on the published binary before claiming all controls matched. This run's immutable result remains inconclusive. Full automatic hauling/convergence is still separate unfinished verification.

## BG02 and automatic delivery — corrected fixture prepared for runtime

- BG02 was extended from the accepted BG01 fixture with the actual ordinary `CookMealSimpleBulk` recipe, 20 rice plus 20 potatoes, and one native four-unit meal product. Its new read-only `RecipeWorker.ConsumeIngredient` observer records unique ingredient identities and quantities after actual product creation and before successful native cleanup. No HD batch mode, replacement job or simulated product creation is used.
- Independent source reviewer `source_inventory` verified installed recipe XML, native GenRecipe/Toils_Recipe/RecipeWorker calls, Harmony argument behavior and delivery metadata additions. Review found missing observer cleanup on early cooking-test exit.
